Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in bio theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 771 and the invariant I(A,B) = 711 mod 3.

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the bio structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 681. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 835 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
